# Consensus fork contract

Source paths are relative to the repository root. Declaration tables identify the interfaces in the source files; runtime behavior is defined by their implementations.

## autobahn-events

| Interface | Kind | Source | Declaration |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| <code>interface.15.evt-autobahn-executed-block-events-j57</code> | <code>event</code> | <code>consensus/internal/p2p/giga_router.go</code> | <code>tm.event='NewBlockHeader' and tm.event='Tx' published by GigaRouter.executeBlock</code> |
| <code>interface.15.go-consensus-internal-p2p-block-event-publisher-j57</code> | <code>go_exported</code> | <code>consensus/internal/p2p/giga_router.go</code> | <code>type BlockEventPublisher interface {</code> |
| <code>interface.15.go-consensus-internal-p2p-giga-router-set-event-bus-j57</code> | <code>go_exported</code> | <code>consensus/internal/p2p/giga_router.go</code> | <code>func (r *GigaRouter) SetEventBus(bus BlockEventPublisher)</code> |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | GigaRouter.SetEventBus(BlockEventPublisher) in consensus/internal/p2p/giga_router.go and its publication from executeBlock at consensus/internal/p2p/giga_router.go |
| storage | No storage; events feed the indexer service and its kv EventSink |
| errors | Publish errors are logged and never returned from executeBlock |
| events | tm.event='NewBlockHeader' with EventDataNewBlockHeader and tm.event='Tx' with EventDataTx, the types of consensus/types/events.go |
| gas | Not metered |
| invariants | Events of height h are published after SaveFinalizeBlockResponses(h) and before PushAppHash(h); one Tx event per tx in block order |
| pause | No pause |

| Requirement | Behavior |
| --- | --- |
| <code>req.15273</code> | Under Autobahn every executed tx publishes one Tx event with its height, index and result, the input the tx indexer consumes. |
| <code>req.15276</code> | Under Autobahn block_search indexes executed blocks through the NewBlockHeader events of the router. |

| <code>interface.15.go-consensus-cmd-tendermint-commands-err-autobahn-offline-store-j57</code> | <code>go_exported</code> | <code>consensus/cmd/tendermint/commands/reindex_event.go</code> | <code>ErrAutobahnOfflineStore = errors.New("offline block and state store tooling is not supported when autobahn-config-file is set")</code> |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | Offline consensus CLI commands of consensus/cmd/tendermint/commands: reset blockchain, reindex-event, inspect, and the LoadTendermintState and RollbackStateToTargetHeight functions of consensus/cmd/tendermint/commands/rollback.go |
| storage | data/autobahn is the default Autobahn persistent state directory (consensus/cmd/tendermint/commands/gen_autobahn_config.go); CometBFT stores under data/ and data/tendermint/ |
| errors | ErrAutobahnOfflineStore when autobahn-config-file is set |
| events | None |
| gas | Not applicable |
| activation | Refusal is decided by Config.AutobahnConfigFile at consensus/config/config.go |
| invariants | No offline tool opens CometBFT stores of an Autobahn node; reset blockchain removes data/autobahn |
| pause | No pause |

| Requirement | Behavior |
| --- | --- |
| <code>req.15269</code> | reindex-event under an Autobahn config fails with ErrAutobahnOfflineStore before reading any store. |
| <code>req.15270</code> | RollbackStateToTargetHeight refuses under an Autobahn config with an error naming the rollback; its caller at sdk/server/rollback.go surfaces it. |
| <code>req.15270</code> | LoadTendermintState refuses under an Autobahn config; its caller at sdk/server/rollback.go surfaces the error. |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | Offline consensus CLI commands of consensus/cmd/tendermint/commands: reset blockchain, reindex-event, inspect, and the LoadTendermintState and RollbackStateToTargetHeight functions of consensus/cmd/tendermint/commands/rollback.go |
| storage | data/autobahn is the default Autobahn persistent state directory (consensus/cmd/tendermint/commands/gen_autobahn_config.go); CometBFT stores under data/ and data/tendermint/ |
| errors | ErrAutobahnOfflineStore when autobahn-config-file is set |
| events | None |
| gas | Not applicable |
| activation | Refusal is decided by Config.AutobahnConfigFile at consensus/config/config.go |
| invariants | No offline tool opens CometBFT stores of an Autobahn node; reset blockchain removes data/autobahn |
| pause | No pause |

## rpc-autobahn

| Interface | Kind | Source | Declaration |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| <code>interface.15.go-consensus-internal-autobahn-data-state-first-block-j57</code> | <code>go_exported</code> | <code>consensus/internal/autobahn/data/state.go</code> | <code>func (s *State) FirstBlock() types.GlobalBlockNumber</code> |
| <code>interface.15.go-consensus-internal-p2p-giga-router-earliest-block-number-j57</code> | <code>go_exported</code> | <code>consensus/internal/p2p/giga_router.go</code> | <code>func (r *GigaRouter) EarliestBlockNumber() int64</code> |
| <code>interface.15.go-consensus-rpc-coretypes-err-consensus-state-not-available-j57</code> | <code>go_exported</code> | <code>consensus/rpc/coretypes/responses.go</code> | <code>ErrConsensusStateNotAvailable = errors.New("consensus round state is not available under autobahn consensus")</code> |
| <code>interface.15.go-consensus-rpc-coretypes-err-evidence-not-accepted-j57</code> | <code>go_exported</code> | <code>consensus/rpc/coretypes/responses.go</code> | <code>ErrEvidenceNotAccepted = errors.New("evidence is not accepted under autobahn consensus")</code> |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | GigaRouter.SetEventBus(BlockEventPublisher) in consensus/internal/p2p/giga_router.go and its publication from executeBlock at consensus/internal/p2p/giga_router.go |
| storage | No storage; events feed the indexer service and its kv EventSink |
| errors | Publish errors are logged and never returned from executeBlock |
| events | tm.event='NewBlockHeader' with EventDataNewBlockHeader and tm.event='Tx' with EventDataTx, the types of consensus/types/events.go |
| gas | Not metered |
| invariants | Events of height h are published after SaveFinalizeBlockResponses(h) and before PushAppHash(h); one Tx event per tx in block order |
| pause | No pause |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | Consensus JSON-RPC routes of consensus/internal/rpc/core/routes.go served by Environment under Autobahn: status, lag_status, blockchain, header, header_by_hash, commit, tx, tx_search, block_search, consensus_params, dump_consensus_state, consensus_state, broadcast_evidence |
| storage | Reads GigaRouter.BlockByNumber, BlockByHash, LastCommittedBlockNumber and EarliestBlockNumber; the CometBFT BlockStore and StateStore stay unfed except FinalizeBlock responses |
| errors | coretypes.ErrCommitNotAvailable, ErrConsensusStateNotAvailable, ErrEvidenceNotAccepted and ErrHeightNotAvailable from consensus/rpc/coretypes/responses.go |
| events | None emitted by the routes |
| gas | Not metered; JSON-RPC handlers |
| activation | Active whenever env.gigaRouter() at consensus/internal/rpc/core/env.go holds a router |
| invariants | No handler panics under Autobahn; every chain-data handler answers a result or a named sentinel; heights below EarliestBlockNumber answer ErrHeightNotAvailable |
| pause | No pause; Autobahn is selected by autobahn-config-file at consensus/config/config.go |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | Consensus JSON-RPC routes of consensus/internal/rpc/core/routes.go served by Environment under Autobahn: status, lag_status, blockchain, header, header_by_hash, commit, tx, tx_search, block_search, consensus_params, dump_consensus_state, consensus_state, broadcast_evidence |
| storage | Reads GigaRouter.BlockByNumber, BlockByHash, LastCommittedBlockNumber and EarliestBlockNumber; the CometBFT BlockStore and StateStore stay unfed except FinalizeBlock responses |
| errors | coretypes.ErrCommitNotAvailable, ErrConsensusStateNotAvailable, ErrEvidenceNotAccepted and ErrHeightNotAvailable from consensus/rpc/coretypes/responses.go |
| events | None emitted by the routes |
| gas | Not metered; JSON-RPC handlers |
| activation | Active whenever env.gigaRouter() at consensus/internal/rpc/core/env.go holds a router |
| invariants | No handler panics under Autobahn; every chain-data handler answers a result or a named sentinel; heights below EarliestBlockNumber answer ErrHeightNotAvailable |
| pause | No pause; Autobahn is selected by autobahn-config-file at consensus/config/config.go |

| Requirement | Behavior |
| --- | --- |
| <code>req.15252</code> | ErrCommitNotAvailable, ErrConsensusStateNotAvailable and ErrEvidenceNotAccepted are distinct sentinels whose messages contain neither "height is not available" nor the chain-head message, so the light provider string match at consensus/light/provider/http/http.go cannot mistake them for a missing block. |
| <code>req.15254</code> | tx with prove on the classic path answers ErrHeightNotAvailable when the block of an indexed tx is absent from the BlockStore. |
| <code>req.15275</code> | GigaRouter.EarliestBlockNumber reports the lowest block BlockByNumber serves. |
| <code>req.15278</code> | Every consensus RPC handler that reads chain data answers under Autobahn with a result or a named sentinel, never a panic or a silent empty answer. |
